ALLOWED STATUSES: KNOWN THEOREM; PROVED/EXACT; PROVED/RESTRICTED; COMPUTATIONAL; CONDITIONAL; CANDIDATE; OPEN; FALSIFIED/RETIRED. State the exact rule system, model, encoding, quantifiers, scope and assumptions. No finite experiment promotes itself to an all-input lower bound.

TARGET
[P2-00] KNOWN THEOREM | SAT not in deterministic uniform P implies P != NP. Conversely, SAT in P implies P=NP by Cook-Levin NP-completeness. Alternative project spines A and B below each require ALL load-bearing arrows, not just a published restricted lower bound. P != NP remains OPEN.
Owner LSPV-1; falsification LSPV-5.

[P2-D2] CONDITIONAL / FIXED DEFINITIONS | Once R is legally fixed, for UNSAT F define W_R(F) as minimum sum of weights of DISTINCT paid inference nodes over ALL valid source-authenticated refutation DAGs, granting optimal after-the-fact choice of branching, sharing, reconvergence, auxiliary definitions, temporary growth, valid algebra and compact representations. Wbar_R(N)=max{W_R(F):F valid UNSAT and |encode(F)|<=N}; empty max=0. With discrete nonnegative fixed weights and existence of a finite legal proof, min is attained; otherwise explicitly use an infimum.
A split on x is an AND obligation: both F,x |- contradiction and F,not x |- contradiction must be discharged, unless another legal refutation bypasses the split. Different valid proof options form OR alternatives. A branch-local lemma may be shared only with all assumptions proved/discharged. Count shared paid nodes once, not once per consumer. Historical calculus does NOT automatically gain a free SPLIT, WEAKEN or DISCHARGE rule.
Owner LSPV-1/LSPV-3. Evidence: Nov. 2 shared handoff and later issued branching-DAG instructions.
[P2-D2 evidence, LSPV-1/468, 2026-11-07 00:08 PST] PROVED/RESTRICTED in a DISTINCT abstract acyclic unit-paid AND/OR dependency calculus: k required goals, each with a private p-node OR derivation or a p-node common prefix plus one distinct finishing inference, have sum of individually optimal costs kp but global minimum distinct-node union p+k; for p=k the scalar overestimate factor k/2 is unbounded. This is an exact warning that a parameterized branching/shared proof-work formula must optimize the WHOLE legal proof DAG, not each required case separately. Strict original signed proper 3-CNF F = (-x|y|t),(-x|y|-t),(x|-y|u),(x|-y|-u) has 8 SAT models: branch x derives y, incompatible branch -x derives -y, and resolving their units globally would be UNSOUND without valid assumption discharge. The abstract hypergraph is NOT a native R_mix341 accepted refutation and creates NO historical rule. [P2-D2 evidence, LSPV-1/470, 2026-11-07 PST] PROVED/RESTRICTED NP-COMPLETE GLOBAL SHARED-DAG OPTIMIZATION CONTROL IN A FIXED LAYERED SUBGRAMMAR OF ORDINARY RESOLUTION, HISTORICAL ALL-RULE MINIMUM OPEN | For any SET COVER instance U={1,...,k}, S_1,...,S_r with union U, make the signed original general CNF F=(a) AND all (-a OR s_j) AND all (-s_j OR g_i) for i in S_j AND (-g_1 OR ... OR -g_k). Its SAT label is trivially the negation of full coverage, so no SAT decision hardness follows. Restrict candidate original-source-authenticated shared-DAG refutations to three globally specified phases: select and derive some set units s_j once (one RES each), derive all k distinct goals g_i by one RES each from a selected covering set and its original membership clause, and resolve the final negative-goal source with all k units (k RES). Every step is legal ordinary binary Resolution; a set node may be shared among several goals and is paid only once. The exact globally minimized cost WITHIN THIS LAYERED GRAMMAR is W_layer(F)=2k+tau(U,S), where tau is minimum set-cover cardinality; threshold <=2k+b is NP-complete by Karp's SET COVER reduction despite W_layer<=2k+r and polynomial-time source UNSAT recognition. This does NOT establish NP-hardness of the unrestricted ordinary Resolution minimum, historical R_mix341 optimization, a 3-CNF hardness transfer, or SAT computation. Symbolic reduction; exact published-JS V8 replay of 57,164 finite set systems, 105,218 assertions, 1,502 source-authenticated refutations, 235 finite both-label CNF controls and nine corruption rejections PASS. Evidence: research/agents/lspv-1/2026-11-07_LSPV1_SET_COVER_SHARED_RES_OPT470.md; experiments/arc/lspv1_470_layered_setcover_resolution.cjs; results/raw/arc/2026-11-07_LSPV1_470_SET_COVER_SHARED_RES_RAW.json; research/coordination/2026-11-07_LSPV1_SET_COVER_DAG470_FIVE_ROLE_HANDOFF.md. The global free optimal-derivation relaxation and distinction between minimum work and actual proof-search cost remain mandatory. [P2-D1], [P2-B1], [P2-B2], [P2-C3] remain OPEN.

OR -g_k) and minimum covering set selection J of size tau, begin from the original all-negative-goal clause; resolve once on each g_i against an original membership clause for its covering j(i), reaching OR_{j in J}(-s_j) in k paid steps, then resolve against original (-a OR s_j) once for each selected j, reaching -a in tau paid steps, and finally resolve original (a) to false in one step. Every pivot, parent, source ID, and conclusion is ordinary syntactic binary Resolution. Hence W_full,Res(F)<=k+tau+1, strictly below the PREVIOUS EXACT RESTRICTED-LAYER minimum W_layer=2k+tau by k-1 for k>=2. In singleton set-cover S_1=U, the source H_k has m=k+3 original clauses, EACH separately indispensable by explicit deleted-clause SAT witnesses; every sound source-explicit unit-paid at-most-binary whole-DAG proof must use q>=m-1=k+2 nodes, and the backward Resolution chain attains this FULL DECLARED-SEMANTIC minimum even with permitted binary mixed rules, shared DAGs and conservative paid definitions: W_full,binary(H_k)=k+2 vs W_layer(H_k)=2k+1 (ratio -> 2). ROUTE A: FLMV SAME-MACHINE CONTRACTION
[P2-A1] KNOWN THEOREM / MODEL AUDIT | Use the precise published FLMV time-space lower-bound theorem ONLY for its specific uniform deterministic, indexed random-access input, and same-machine resource model. Verify the exact statement and exponent conventions before applying. No machine conversion without paying simulation overhead.
Owner LSPV-4.
[P2-A2] OPEN, LOAD-BEARING | For EVERY uniform total correct polynomial-time SAT decider A, irrespective of unknown finite exponents (t,s), construct an effective recursively iterable SAME-MACHINE ALL-INPUT transformation that is SAT/UNSAT/malformed-aware and fully charged, with:
    t' <= max(11/10, 3/5+t/2)
    s' <= max(1/2, s/2)
including child materialization rho, output, source/index reads, guest A costs, failed branches, joins and all-phase space bound s'<=max(rho,eta,lambda,lambda*s). No unrestricted source splitter, semantic oracle, hardcoded answers or per-N nonuniform choice.
Owners: LSPV-2 construction, LSPV-4 resources; LSPV-5 audit.

Native stack realization is an EXPLICIT hypothesis, not proved for generic Q. This extends the previously recognized source-only generic-splitter firewall to adaptivity over oracle ANSWERS; it does not exclude solver-intensional code/trace-dependent contraction. [P2-A2 evidence, LSPV-2/479, 2026-11-07 16:18 PST] PROVED/RESTRICTED (standard constructive recursion-theorem method), CONDITIONAL SAT APPLICATION, NOT A UNIVERSAL CONTRACTION NO-GO | For any fixed L with a total correct polynomial-time decider A0, NO partial computable E guaranteed to halt on EVERY correct polynomial-time L-decider code can return a universally valid worst-case polynomial runtime exponent k. Intensional self-reference yields a correct polytime D which on length n simulates E(code(D)) for only n steps, pads >=(n+1)^(k+2) actual transitions if it returned k, then runs A0. D is polynomial regardless of E halting; its correctness forces E to halt under the promise, then contradicts its returned exponent. This applies to SAT ONLY conditionally on SAT in P. It rules out a numerical exponent-extraction prerequisite for Route A, NOT symbolic unknown-exponent A->Phi(A), native all-input contraction, recursive guest closure or any P!=NP conclusion. Independent finite JS 884 and Python 542 checks including 16 original strict signed K4 Tseitin charges (8 SAT/8 UNSAT); no native FLMV execution. [P2-A3] CONDITIONAL / ARITHMETIC VERIFIED | If [P2-A2] exists and iterates indefinitely under the precise FLMV model, the exponent recurrence reaches t*=6/5 and s*=1/2. beta(c)=((c+2)/c^2-c)/2; beta(6/5)=23/45, leaving 1/90 gap over 1/2. An appropriately strict contradiction with FLMV would rule out SAT in P. Arithmetic is not a proof of [P2-A2].

[P2-A4 evidence, LSPV-2/465, 2026-11-07 02:12 PST] PROVED/RESTRICTED **unknown-guest-exponent-oblivious** WORK-index summary extension of LSPV-4/463. [P2-F9] now strengthens the random strict-3-CNF anchor through arbitrary-field PC and bounded-DNF PCR^q lower bounds, but no theorem translates the additional historical native rules into that system. [P2-L5-402] proves a restricted upper-bound control: one explicitly context-labelled Boolean split can be eliminated inside native PC with length at most 3(L0+L1)+1, monomial size at most 4(S0+S1)+2, and degree at most max(d0,d1)+1; an explicit context-expanded PCR decision tree also flattens with polynomial overhead in its displayed tree certificate. [P2-L5-403] gives the complementary reconvergence barrier: outside characteristic two, the canonical multilinear ordinary-PC guard for the even contexts of a linear-size two-state parity search DAG has exactly 2^k monomials, while over GF(2) it has k+1. This refutes one naive polynomial state-DAG-to-guard compilation, but extensions, factored circuits, PCR alternatives, GF(2), and arbitrary mixed proofs remain legal shortcuts. [P2-L5-404] supplies the mandatory upper-bound control: the same guard has an exact O(k)-size acyclic recursive product-extension representation outside characteristic two and an O(k)-size XOR-prefix strict-3CNF circuit; historical proof-side acceptance and source-preserving transfer remain OPEN. [P2-F10] further records that the strongest located finite-field PC+Ext lower bounds are author-corrected to pairwise-disjoint, nonrecursive extension supports and therefore do not cover overlapping or nested historical paid definitions. [P2-L5-401] proves that the direct clone-disjointization repair does not enter the corrected theorem: without equalities the represented functions change; equality extensions retain the shared support and make downstream gates recursive; source equalities change the published hard source. [P2-F11] records a stronger arbitrary-depth algebraic extension lower-bound control whose nonpropositional BVP source has no lower-bound-preserving strict-3CNF or historical-node translation. The all-rule minimum remains OPEN.

[P2-B3] OPEN (STRONGER OPTIONAL) | For EACH fixed uniform total correct decider A, T_A^wc(N)=max_{|x|<=N}T_A(x).
  B1: forall A exists polynomial p_A forall UNSAT F:
      W_R(F) <= p_A(|encode(F)|+T_A(F)).
  B2: forall A exists polynomial p_A forall N:
      Wbar_R(N) <= p_A(N+T_A^wc(N)).
  B3: SAT in P => Wbar_R is polynomial for this SAME R.
B1 => B2 => B3. Pointwise recognizer + exponential fallback can refute B1 for ordinary Resolution without refuting B2 or B3.

[P2-B7] PROVED/RESTRICTED; HISTORICAL B3 OPEN | Fixed-input rejecting computation trace is a satisfiable, disjoint-variable conservative CNF gadget G(C,F), because hardwired encoded input bits and circuit wires are independent of the SAT witness variables of F. For EVERY UNSAT F and EVERY SAT G on disjoint variable sets, the exact minimum distinct unit-paid shared-DAG ordinary Resolution work obeys S_RES(F AND G)=S_RES(F): any refutation restricted to a satisfying G assignment yields an F refutation with no more paid nodes; conversely ignore G. The published Chvatal-Szemeredi density-six proper signed random 3-CNF exponential ordinary Resolution lower bound therefore survives this disjoint trace padding in ordinary Resolution. Gate-output units themselves admit <=2g native Resolution inferences for a g-gate fan-in<=2 deterministic circuit, but combining a rejecting output with an unlicensed opposite-output premise only refutes a DIFFERENT augmented source. This does not establish ANY proof lower bound in the historical full R_mix341; its paid definitions, GF(2), LOCAL-CONVERT, assumption scope, and branch/reconvergence rules are not shown restriction-stable. For B3 the missing implication remains source-authenticated decider correctness -> polynomial same-R refutation. [P2-B8] PROVED/RESTRICTED (ordinary shared-DAG Resolution only; B3 OPEN) | Literal-alias conservative padding extension to [P2-B7]. For ANY UNSAT original-variable CNF F(X) and ANY CNF G(X,Y) with a SINGLE globally consistent substitution sigma fixing X and mapping each private Y variable to an original signed literal or Boolean constant, with every substituted G clause tautological on ALL X assignments, the exact minimum distinct unit-paid source-authenticated binary Resolution work satisfies S_Res(F AND G)=S_Res(F). Proof: project the entire shared DAG topologically once; every original RES inference under signed literal substitution either becomes tautological, contracts to a previously derived stronger parent, or becomes ONE legal RES inference on the substituted pivot. All G source images vanish. No paid sharing is duplicated. This generalizes disjoint SAT trace padding and covers shared-X literal aliases, but NOT nonlinear Skolem functions, Extended Resolution, paid GF(2)/AND3/LOCAL-CONVERT/DEF, historical accepted-byte R_mix341, B1/B2/B3 or arbitrary-machine SAT cost. [P2-B9] PROVED/RESTRICTED (ordinary unrestricted shared-DAG Resolution ONLY; historical R_mix341 and B3 OPEN) | Quantitative nonlinear conservative Skolem-section proof transfer (LSPV-1/465). For EVERY UNSAT original CNF F(X) and auxiliary CNF G(X,Y) that has ONE total leaf-constant Skolem section on a full binary decision tree T over original X, with L leaves and with EACH restricted G clause a tautology at each leaf, the minimum distinct paid binary Resolution inferences obey S_Res(F) <= L*(S_Res(F AND G)+1)-1, equivalently S_Res(F AND G) >= (S_Res(F)+1)/L-1. Proof: restrict any shared RES proof to each leaf's original-variable path and auxiliary assignment, deleting all G clauses; replay each restricted proof using ORIGINAL F sources, retaining only branch-false literals; discharge both child branches by at most ONE correctly oriented original-variable RES per internal node. At most L copies of a minimum augmented proof plus L-1 joins, and any reconvergent sharing can only reduce the distinct-node count. No SPLIT, WEAKEN, DISCHARGE or semantic-oracle rule is added. If a section jointly depends on d original bits, L<=2^d; acquiring a minimum tree is NOT free in algorithmic time. [P2-F8] KNOWN THEOREM / STRICT 3-CNF / TRANSFER OPEN | Chvatal-Szemeredi random proper 3-CNF. Fix k=3 and any positive integer c with c*2^(-3)>=0.7 (for example c=6). On variables X={x_1,...,x_n}, draw m=cn clauses independently and uniformly from the binom(n,3)*2^3 ordinary width-three clauses: choose three distinct variables and independently choose their literal signs. Repeated clauses are permitted because the source is an indexed family. Their 1988 theorem states that there exists epsilon=epsilon(c,3)>0 such that, with probability tending to one as n tends to infinity, F is UNSAT and every ordinary shared-DAG Resolution refutation has at least (1+epsilon)^n clauses. Size parameter: n variables, m=cn source clauses, and Theta(n log n) bits under binary variable identifiers. Model/rules: ordinary Resolution only, with sources or earlier clauses and the empty-clause goal; no native XOR, extension definitions, semantic LOCAL-CONVERT, or branching-discharge rule. Certified status: published high-probability proof-size hardness in ordinary Resolution, not a certificate for a sampled instance, not empirical solver difficulty, and not hardness for all algorithms. Known controls: Cook-style extension variables, algebraic/XOR reasoning, CDCL/preprocessing, shared branching, reconvergence, and every legal R_mix341 rule remain admissible shortcuts in W_R. Remaining transfer: prove a lower-bound-preserving simulation/retraction covering ALL historical native R_mix341 rules and exact source authentication, or this family does not establish [P2-B1]. Primary source: V. Chvatal and E. Szemeredi, "Many Hard Examples for Resolution", JACM 35(4), 1988, Definition and Theorem in Section 2.

[P2-F9] KNOWN THEOREM + PROVED/EXACT DISTRIBUTION PORT / SAME RANDOM STRICT 3-CNF / FULL-MIXED TRANSFER OPEN | For the exact indexed family in [P2-F8], Alekhnovich--Razborov Definition 4.6 and Corollary 4.7 use m=Delta*n clauses drawn independently and uniformly with repetitions from the binom(n,k)*2^k proper signed k-clauses. Encode C=l_1 OR l_2 OR l_3 as p_C=product_{l_i=x_j}(1-x_j)*product_{l_i=not x_j}x_j=0, with Boolean axioms x_j^2-x_j=0. Their theorem gives, over an arbitrary field, PC degree Omega(n/(Delta^(2/(k-2))*log Delta)) with probability 1-o(1); at k=3, Delta=6 this is Omega(n). PC rules are scalar addition and multiplication by one variable. Impagliazzo--Pudlak--Sgall Theorem 5.2 converts this linear degree bound for degree-three sources into exp(Omega(n)) total nonzero-monomial occurrences. Galesi--Lauria Theorem 2 further gives, over fields of characteristic not 2, size greater than 2^(n^(1-o(1))) with high probability in PCR^q for q=o(sqrt(log n/log log n)); PCR^q combines PC/PCR with bounded q-DNF k-monomials, and its Lemma 5 supplies the lower-bound-preserving source-refutation to associated degree-three mod-2-system refutation direction. This is a published combined daglike subsystem bound, not historical R_mix341 hardness. Reproduction port: CNFgen commit 24e633b samples without repetition, but conditioning the published iid distribution on no collision gives that distribution, and total variation is at most binom(6n,2)/(8*binom(n,3))=O(1/n). Thus every 1-o(1) property transfers asymptotically; no individual generated instance is thereby certified UNSAT or proof-size hard. Remaining transfer: prove a polynomial backtranslation of EVERY legal historical source-authenticated mixed branching/shared DAG, including GF(2), LOCAL-CONVERT, AND3, paid definitions/extensions, reconvergence and assumption scope, into a covered system. Evidence: research/main/2026-11-06_1850_MAIN_RANDOM3CNF_PCR_PORT_REFEREE399.md and experiments/review/lspv5_399_random3cnf_port_check.py.

Their F^SEL source (Definitions 6-7) has O(n log n) Boolean variables and polynomially many width-O(log n) constraints built from a robust k-CSP plus binary selector/pigeon variables. The published Theorem 1 states, for M=n*polylog(n) and kappa=O(log log n), size 2^Omega(n/polylog n); Theorem 2 gives a subexponential lower bound for larger M and kappa. Hence the usable theorem does not cover even arity-two overlapping supports S_i={x_0,x_i}, much less recursive/nested definitions. The source is not strict 3-CNF, and no lower-bound-preserving width-reduction into historical R_mix341 is supplied. The paragraph does NOT explicitly exempt all INDEX tapes from space. The author survey (van Melkebeek, Foundations and Trends TCS 2007 §2.1 pp.214-215) repeats this wording and identifies INDEX as a sequential-access tape. An explicit fixed machine copies arbitrary n-bit input to sequential INDEX in O(n) steps touching O(1) non-index WORK; 2^n terminal INDEX words require >=n explicit bits. Thus if INDEX is completely free and s=WORK-only, the unconditional O(s) explicit configuration claim fails; if sequential INDEX high-water is charged in s, an INDEX-only B^k prologue has guest s>=B^k, so LSPV-2/471 and LSPV-4/470-471's low-guest-space transfer premise does NOT automatically apply. [P2-A4 restricted native-INPUT index-head-swap feasibility evidence, LSPV-4/474, 2026-11-07 10:05 PST]
**PROVED/RESTRICTED, for one head-faithful no-cache primitive ONLY.** Under an explicitly assumed head-preserving native indexed-INPUT INDEX-erasure convention, suppose a guest has positioned its sequential INDEX head at H and makes q blank-address indexed INPUT reads.
